### [1] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时序逻辑(ptSTL)进行认证运行时监控。提出了两种可重用监控器：语义基础监控器（通过有限字典的时序原子鲁棒性分数作为预测目标，保证单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口，单次共形校准即可认证整个公式片段）和滚动预测监控器（仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史）。实验表明，滚动监控器在短时域上更紧致，而语义基础监控器在长时域上紧致度提升4倍，并在Waymo数据上验证了共形覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时监控方法在处理视觉观测下的部分可观测性时，通常需要针对每个公式单独训练和校准，缺乏可重用性。本文旨在设计一种可重用的监控框架，使得一次训练和校准后即可认证目标片段内的任意公式。

Method: 1) 提出语义基础(Semantic Basis)概念，证明对于由有限时序原子字典诱导的公式片段，原子鲁棒性分数向量是单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口的最小预测目标；2) 设计基于解析树的确定性解码器评估公式；3) 采用单次共形校准(Conformal Calibration)认证整个片段，无需联合界；4) 提出滚动预测监控器(Rolling Prediction Monitor)，仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构历史。

Result: 在行人过街基准测试中，滚动监控器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，而语义基础监控器在长时域上紧致度提升最多4倍。在Waymo真实驾驶数据上，两种监控器均经验性地满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 语义基础监控器在长时域上具有显著优势，滚动监控器更适合短时域场景，两者在实际驾驶数据中均能提供有效的认证保证，验证了所提可重用监控框架的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [2] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对三种EEG Transformer(SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM)应用TopK稀疏自编码器提取稀疏特征字典，通过临床分类标准评估单义性和纠缠性，并引入概念引导框架量化引导选择性，揭示"破球"干预和临床纠缠等表征失败问题，最后通过频谱解码器将潜在操作映射为生理可解释的频率特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务中表现优异，但其内部计算过程不透明，阻碍了临床信任的建立。现有方法无法解释这些模型如何编码临床相关概念，也无法进行可控的概念操作。

Method: 使用TopK稀疏自编码器(SAEs)从三种架构不同的EEG Transformer(SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM)中提取稀疏特征字典；基于临床分类标准(异常、年龄、性别、用药)评估单义性和纠缠性；提出"目标vs.非目标"探针区域指标量化概念引导的选择性；利用频谱解码器将潜在操作映射到幅度谱。

Result: 单一超参数程序可稳健迁移至三种架构；概念引导存在三种操作模式(可选择引导、编码但纠缠、未编码)；发现了"破球"干预(全局模型性能崩溃)和临床纠缠(如年龄-病理混杂)等关键表征失败；成功将操作映射为生理可解释的频率特征(如病理性慢波抑制和α频带恢复)。

Conclusion: 提出的框架揭示了EEG基础模型的表征局限性和概念纠缠问题，为增强模型可解释性和临床可信度提供了系统化分析工具，并通过频谱解码桥接了潜在操作与生理信号之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [3] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需手动调参的架构退火联合VAE（Joint-VAE），通过将编码器输出进行架构级别的退火处理，在7,416个结构连接组数据集上实现了对dMRI采集相关变异（扫描仪、协议差异）与生物变异的无监督分离，ARI达到0.53，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI数据在不同站点、扫描仪和协议下采集时存在显著差异，这种采集相关变异会干扰结构连接组分析。传统降维方法将所有变异建模为连续空间，导致采集效应被吸收进连续潜变量；而现有的混合潜空间模型虽引入离散成分，但需要手动调节容量参数以保证离散成分捕获到目标变异，缺乏自适应机制。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE框架，在编码器输出后进行架构级别的退火（architectural annealing），使模型在训练过程中自适应地平衡离散和连续潜变量，无需手动调节容量参数。构建包含N=7,416个结构连接组的数据集（年龄2-102岁，13个研究，25种采集参数组合），对比标准VAE、PCA+k-means聚类、以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。

Result: 架构退火方法在站点学习任务上ARI=0.53（p<0.05），显著优于所有基线方法。联合连续-离散潜空间能够有效恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类结构。

Conclusion: 架构级别的退火机制提供了一种有效的无监督手段来捕获dMRI中的采集变异：通过联合建模平滑结构和类别结构，Joint-VAE能够在无监督条件下自动分离采集相关变异与生物变异，无需手动调参。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [4] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型后训练中奖励最大化导致轨迹锁定（概率质量过度集中到少数去噪路径）的问题，提出TraFL方法，通过轨迹平衡目标将策略导向奖励倾斜的目标分布，在数学推理和代码生成任务中始终优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型的后训练方法大多采用奖励最大化目标，但这会导致"轨迹锁定"失效模式——奖励驱动的更新将概率质量过度集中到有限的去噪路径上，降低了重复采样时覆盖其他正确解的能力。

Method: 提出TraFL（Trajectory Flow baLancing），采用轨迹平衡目标，将策略训练向一个以冻结参考模型为锚点的奖励倾斜目标分布。具体包括：扩散兼容的序列级替代目标和学习到的prompt依赖归一化项。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有评测长度设置下均优于基线的后训练方法，且增益随采样预算增加而持续。在Minerva Math上保持优于基线，在LiveCodeBench所有难度划分上表现最强。

Conclusion: 轨迹平衡目标能有效缓解扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，实现更鲁棒的性能提升。

Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

</details>


### [5] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习的框架，使多个机构能在不共享私有数据的情况下联合微调大语言模型（LLM），并在医疗和金融领域的四个数据集上验证了该方法在非独立同分布（non-IID）设定下的有效性。实验结果表明，联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，优于孤立单机构学习，且QLoRA和IA3等参数高效微调策略在绿色AI方面具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的发展依赖公开数据，但许多最有价值的数据（如医疗和金融领域的私有数据）分布在多个机构中，受隐私、法规和组织壁垒限制无法共享。同时这些数据集存在非独立同分布（non-IID）特性，直接利用这些数据训练LLM面临挑战。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台，构建了一个联邦协作框架，使各节点在不交换私有数据的情况下联合微调共享LLM。采用三种参数高效微调（PEFT）策略——LoRA、QLoRA和IA3，在非IID设定下（模拟机构数据异质性）进行实验，涉及医疗（MedQA, MedMCQA）和金融（FPB, FiQA-SA）四个问答与分类数据集。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，且优于孤立单机构学习。从绿色AI角度，QLoRA和IA3在精度损失有限的情况下提升了效率，表明联邦PEFT是适应LLM处理不可共享数据的可行方法。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调是在隐私保护前提下解锁分布于各机构的私有数据、提升LLM领域专业性和实际效用的有效途径。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [6] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将加权InfoNCE目标函数解释为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习中权重方案与嵌入几何之间的精确关系。研究表明：有监督对比学习(SupCon)在类别平衡时恢复经典正则单形体几何，但类别不平衡会破坏对称性；而Soft SupCon则能保持正则单形体几何。在连续标签场景下，y-Aware CL存在几何不一致性，而欧几里得-欧几里得加权或X-CLR则能获得唯一最优嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习旨在保留样本间的关系结构，但所产生嵌入的几何特性仍未被充分理解。本文旨在通过距离几何视角，精确刻画不同权重方案下对比学习最优嵌入的几何结构。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数重新解释为距离几何问题(DGP)，通过分析目标函数的最优条件，推导出不同监督和弱监督设置下最优嵌入的精确几何特征。

Result: 1) SupCon在类别平衡时恢复正则单形体几何，类别不平衡导致非均匀类间相似度；2) Soft SupCon无论类别是否平衡都保持正则单形体几何；3) y-Aware CL在连续标签下存在几何不一致性，除非标签位于超球面上；4) 欧几里得-欧几里得加权和X-CLR能获得唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 权重方案的选择决定了对比学习在几何上是否可实现、退化或不一致，为设计对比学习目标函数提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [7] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior，一种基于近期动作历史构建的简单时间先验，替代标准高斯源分布用于扩散/流匹配的机器人控制，能够显著提高操作任务成功率，其效果源于更直的路径（类似Rectified Flow中的最优传输耦合），并在先验空间强化学习中提升样本效率和最终性能，揭示了源分布作为生成式机器人控制中一个重要的设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散和流匹配的生成式机器人控制策略通常使用标准高斯分布作为源分布，这一选择可能并非最优，源分布作为设计维度尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出WarmPrior，利用最近的动作历史构建一个时间上具有接地性的简单先验，替代高斯源分布用于扩散/流匹配的生成式控制策略。

Result: WarmPrior在机器人操作任务上持续提升成功率，产生更直的概率路径（类似Rectified Flow的最优传输耦合效果），并在先验空间强化学习中同时改善样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但尚未充分探索的设计维度，WarmPrior作为一种简单有效的方法证明了这一点。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [8] [An Amortized Efficiency Threshold for Comparing Neural and Heuristic Solvers in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14624)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对“神经组合优化求解器比CPU元启发式算法能耗更高”的常见批评，指出其逻辑谬误在于将训练成本直接等同于净效率低下。作者提出摊销效率阈值（AET）框架，定义神经求解器在总能耗或碳排放上与启发式基线打平所需的部署量，并证明当神经求解器在单实例上胜出时，累计能耗比趋于一个严格小于1的常数。在MTVRP环境（n=20，19个问题变体）中，交叉点约为1.58×10^5个实例，单实例能耗比为0.41。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见批评认为神经组合优化求解器因GPU训练成本而比CPU元启发式算法能耗更高，但这一批评忽略了训练成本是一次性固定投入，而元启发式算法的推理成本是每次部署重复发生的。本文旨在纠正这一逻辑错误，提出一个可量化的框架来公平比较两者的能源效率。

Method: 提出摊销效率阈值（AET）框架，定义神经求解器与启发式基线在总能耗或碳排放上打平所需的部署量。证明在神经求解器单实例胜出的条件下，累计能耗比趋于常数（<1），且不依赖于训练成本的测量方式。加入硬件制造中的隐含碳项，对称处理双方。在MTVRP环境（n=20，19个问题变体，5个训练种子）中，以HGS via PyVRP为基线进行实验。

Result: 交叉点约为1.58×10^5个部署实例；单实例能耗比为0.41（反映实例规模适中）。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于提出AET框架、开源工具和测量协议；更大问题规模下能耗比的结构收敛性留待未来实证研究。

Abstract: A common critique of neural combinatorial-optimization solvers is that they are less energy-efficient than CPU metaheuristics, given the operational energy cost of training them on GPUs. This paper examines the inferential step from "training is expensive" to "neural solvers are net-inefficient", which is where the critique actually goes wrong. Training the network costs a large fixed amount of GPU energy; running the metaheuristic costs a small amount of CPU energy on every instance, repeated as long as the solver is deployed. The two are not commensurable until a deployment volume is fixed. We define the Amortized Efficiency Threshold (AET) as the deployment volume above which a neural solver breaks even with a heuristic baseline in total energy or carbon, under an explicit constraint on solution quality. We show that the cumulative-energy ratio between the two solvers tends to a constant strictly below one whenever the network wins per-instance, and that this limit does not depend on how the training cost was measured. An embodied-carbon term amortizes hardware fabrication symmetrically on both sides. We instantiate the framework on the Multi-Task VRP (MTVRP) environment at n=20 customers across 19 problem variants and five training seeds, with HGS via PyVRP as the heuristic baseline. The measured crossover sits near $1.58 \times 10^5$ deployed instances; the per-instance ratio is 0.41, reflecting the moderate size of the instances tested. The contribution is the framework, the open instrumentation, and the measurement protocol; structural convergence of the ratio at larger problem sizes is left to future empirical work.

</details>


### [9] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全面的能耗核算框架，通过详细的阶段跟踪GPU设备功耗来测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本，揭示了传统蒸馏评估中忽略的教师端能耗（如数据生成、logit缓存和评估），并基于实证分析给出了能耗约束下的蒸馏方法选择设计规则，同时开源了标准化的测量工具和核算协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的部署导致GPU需求和数据中心规模激增，引发电力消耗和电网压力等担忧。虽然蒸馏常被视为获取更便宜、更高效模型的有效途径，但现有评估很少考虑完整的端到端能耗和资源成本，特别是教师端的工作负载（如数据生成、logit缓存和评估），导致对蒸馏的真实能效认识不全面。

Method: 提出了一种全面的能耗核算框架，通过详细的阶段跟踪GPU设备功耗来测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本。实验中分离并记录了不同阶段的实证能耗，系统测量了两种蒸馏方法（经典的基于logit的知识蒸馏和基于合成数据的监督微调）的能耗和排放，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 揭示了传统蒸馏评估中之前被忽略的教师端成本，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿，给出了在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则，并开源了标准化测量工具和核算协议。

Conclusion: 蒸馏的真实能耗成本需要完整端到端核算，不能忽略教师端开销；提出的框架和协议为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供了标准化基础，确保对完整管道能耗影响负责。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [10] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一个面向表格数据的预训练基础模型，通过合成数据训练扩展到百万行级数据集，在标准表格基准上以单次前向传播显著超越所有调参和集成基线，同时引入测试时计算缩放，推理速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍，且支持时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据等新模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据支撑着科学和工业中大多数高价值预测问题，但现有方法在规模、速度和跨模态泛化能力上存在局限。TabPFN已推动了该领域的基础模型革命，但用户反馈表明仍需在更大数据集（百万行级）上提升性能并大幅降低训练和推理时间。

Method: TabPFN-3完全在来自先验的合成数据上进行预训练，引入行分块（row-chunking）和缩减的KV缓存以扩展到100万行，并首次在表格基础模型中引入测试时计算缩放（test-time compute scaling）。此外提供专用检查点TabPFN-TS-3用于时间序列任务。

Result: 在TabArena基准上，TabPFN-3单次前向传播显著优于所有调参和集成基线；在速度/性能前沿上占帕累托主导。TabPFN-3-Plus（Thinking）在TabArena上以200+ Elo击败所有非TabPFN模型（最大数据子集上达420 Elo），比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍。在RelBenchV1上达到新SOTA，在TabSTAR上达到SOTA，fev-bench排名第2。SHAP值计算快120倍，推理速度比TabPFN-2.5快20倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3通过合成数据预训练、测试时计算缩放和高效架构扩展，显著推动了表格预测的前沿，在多个模态和规模上实现了SOTA性能，同时保持极快的推理速度，为表格基础模型设立了新标准。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [11] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏MoE层中三个专家可能两两兼容但三者合并时形成不可简化循环，这是现有基于两两信号评分压缩器的结构性盲点。通过2-复形的调和核与Hodge分解精确定位该障碍，提出HodgeCover方法覆盖关键边和三角，在激进专家压缩场景下达到最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习无关的MoE压缩方法仅基于两两专家间的兼容性进行评分，忽略了三个专家虽两两兼容却无法共同合并的循环障碍，导致压缩效果受限。

Method: 将障碍建模为以专家为顶点、KL散度为边的2-复形上的调和核，通过Hodge分解分离边缘信号，提出HodgeCover贪婪覆盖调和关键边与三角，并设计混合变体结合权重剪枝。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE模型上，HodgeCover在专家压缩维度匹配SOTA，在混合激进压缩前沿领先，且在所有四个Hodge分量上维持独特的质量平衡。

Conclusion: 揭示MoE结构的调和核改变了在关键压缩场景下最优压缩器的选择，证明高阶结构信息对学习无关压缩至关重要。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [12] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中竞争力日益增强，但去噪目标如何组织学习尚不明确。本文证明精确反向过程在粗粒度支持信息与细粒度频率信息之间建立层次结构。对于均匀扩散和吸收扩散，在小噪声区间的最终去噪步骤中，每个单token反向编辑分解为：主导尺度（由是否移向数据支持决定）和更精细的系数（决定同一尺度内的相对概率）。因此，恢复有效性结构只需学习反向概率的正确数量级，而恢复数据频率需要系数级估计。实验支持这些预测：支持定位早于频率排序，两种扩散机制的速率分离与理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解离散扩散模型中去噪目标如何组织学习过程，特别是模型如何在不同粒度（支持结构与频率分布）上恢复数据分布。

Method: 理论分析：证明均匀和吸收扩散在小噪声区间内，每个反向编辑分解为支持尺度（是否移向数据支持）和频率系数（同一尺度内的相对概率）。实验验证：在掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务上测试理论预测。

Result: 支持定位（粗粒度）早于频率排序（细粒度）出现；均匀扩散呈现三分性（有效性改善、保持、恶化），吸收扩散主导质量集中在有效性改善移动上；两种扩散机制的速率分离与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型学习数据支持先于数据频率；恢复有效性结构比恢复数据频率更容易（只需数量级学习而非系数级估计）。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [13] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dywave动态分词框架，利用小波层次分解对IoT传感信号进行自适应压缩和语义边界识别，在五个真实数据集上实现高达12%的准确率提升和75%的输入令牌长度缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统连续采集的异构传感信号具有非平稳、多尺度的特性，标准分词技术难以有效处理这类信号的时序结构和底层物理事件，亟需一种能构建紧凑输入表示且对齐内在时序结构的动态分词方法。

Method: 基于小波层次分解的动态分词方法，通过识别对应语义事件的有意义时间边界，自适应压缩冗余区间并保持时序连贯性。

Result: 在活动识别、压力评估、附近物体检测等五个真实IoT数据集上，准确率比SOTA方法最高提升12%，主流序列模型的输入令牌长度减少高达75%，并展现了更好的域迁移鲁棒性和序列长度适应性。

Conclusion: Dywave作为一种面向IoT传感信号的动态分词框架，能有效构建紧凑且语义对齐的输入表示，同时提升准确率和计算效率，并增强模型对域偏移和变长序列的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [14] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对数据稀缺场景下强化学习高更新倍率（UTD）导致的表征过拟合问题，本文提出R2R2正则化方法，通过非中心化目标修正自预测学习（SPL）中的表征不稳定性，在11个连续控制任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注评论员偏置，但自预测学习（SPL）在高更新倍率（UTD）下的表征层不稳定性尚未被充分研究，需要提出针对性解决方案。

Method: 提出R2R2（Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction）正则化方法。理论分析发现标准零中心化与SPL的谱性质冲突，设计非中心化目标。在SPL原生算法TD7上验证，并为缺乏SPL的SOTA方法SimbaV2集成SPL模块得到SimbaV2-SPL。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上，UTD=20时，R2R2使TD7提升约22%，并在SimbaV2-SPL基础上进一步提升，SimbaV2-SPL本身即达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: R2R2能有效缓解高UTD下的过拟合问题，且与现有方法正交，具备良好的通用性和兼容性。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [15] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SP-KV是一种轻量级效用预测机制，通过动态稀疏化KV缓存（压缩3-10倍），在不损失性能的前提下显著提升长序列生成的内存效率和解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代测试时计算和智能体范式下，语言模型处理越来越长的序列，而Transformer架构中Key-Value缓存的内存占用和带宽成为高效文本生成的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV)：训练一个轻量级效用预测器，对每个键值对打分；近期KV始终通过局部窗口可用，较老的KV对仅在其预测效用超过阈值时才写入缓存并参与全局注意力。LLM与效用预测器通过下一词预测损失联合端到端训练，从预训练检查点适配。

Result: 动态稀疏化将KV缓存大小压缩3-10倍，长序列压缩比更高；显著提升内存使用和解码速度；验证损失和下游任务性能几乎无下降；揭示了结构和层/头特定的稀疏模式。

Conclusion: SP-KV不仅能有效缩减KV缓存，其发现的稀疏模式还可用于指导混合局部-全局注意力架构的设计。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

</details>


### [16] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RMemSafe，一种可靠性门控机制，用于解决持续测试时适应（CTTA）中源模型在退化时仍被盲目锚定（盲锚定）的问题。RMemSafe通过冻结源模型的归一化预测熵动态衰减源耦合项，当源后验趋于均匀时，门控关闭，退化为无源回退损失。实验表明，RMemSafe在9个连续损坏匹配分割细胞中的8个上取得最低错误率，相比ROID+ASR，ResNet-50提升1.05个百分点，ViT-B/16提升0.48个百分点，且对源退化的鲁棒性更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTTA方法（如ROID）在源模型严重退化时（如CCC-Hard上ResNet-50仅约1.3% top-1准确率）仍保持相同的源锚定强度，导致性能崩溃。这种"盲锚定"现象表明需要一种机制来感知源模型的可靠性并相应调整对源信息的依赖。

Method: 提出RMemSafe，作为ROID的扩展，通过冻结源模型的归一化预测熵构建可靠性门控。当源预测熵高（接近均匀分布）时，门控关闭，消除所有显式源耦合项（源锚定和一致性滤波），目标退化为无源回退损失（ROID基础损失+边缘校准）。

Result: 在9个匹配分割连续损坏细胞中，RMemSafe在8个上取得最低错误率，在所有9个上是最优的重置类方法。相比ROID+ASR，ResNet-50提升1.05个百分点，ViT-B/16提升0.48个百分点。源退化扫描实验显示RMemSafe的伤害斜率比ROID+ASR浅1.13倍。

Conclusion: 熵门控能有效检测高熵源崩溃，而非错误地抑制低熵但置信错误的源。RMemSafe实现了源退化场景下的优雅退化，解决了CTTA中的盲锚定问题。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration. Combined with ASR, RMemSafe achieves the lowest error on $8$ of $9$ matched-split continual-corruption cells and is the best reset-based method on all $9$, improving ROID+ASR by $1.05$~pp on ResNet-50 and $0.48$~pp on ViT-B/16. A controlled source-degradation sweep shows a $1.13{\times}$ shallower harm slope than ROID+ASR, consistent with the graceful-decay prediction. The entropy gate detects high-entropy source collapse, not confidently wrong low-entropy sources; this scope is explicitly evaluated and discussed.

</details>


### [17] [SurF: A Generative Model for Multivariate Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14069)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Tejas Balaji,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurF是一个利用时间重标定定理作为可学习双射的生成模型，将不规则多变量事件流转换为i.i.d.指数噪声，通过三种高效累积强度参数化和Transformer编码器实现跨数据集预训练，在6个基准上达到最优或接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则采样的多变量事件流难以生成建模：基于分词的方难以处理间隔变化极大的事件，神经时序点过程受限于窗口级数值积分。

Method: (i) 利用时间重标定定理作为可学习的双射，将事件序列映射为独立同分布的指数噪声；(ii) 三种高效的累积强度参数化方法以扩展到长序列；(iii) 基于Transformer的编码器实现多数据集预训练。

Result: 在6个真实世界基准上，SurF在Earthquake、Retweet和Taobao上取得最佳报告时间RMSE，在其余3个上达到试验级噪声范围内的性能。严格留一法评估中，在5/6数据集上超越所有经典和神经自回归基线，在Amazon和Earthquake上超越所有基线。

Conclusion: 该工作向异步事件流的基础模型迈出了初步一步，展示了跨数据集预训练的可行性。

Abstract: Irregularly sampled multivariate event streams remain a stubbornly difficult modality for generative modeling: tokenization-based approaches break down when inter-event intervals vary by orders of magnitude, and neural temporal point processes are bottlenecked by window-level numerical quadrature. We (i) propose SurF, a generative model that uses the Time Rescaling Theorem (TRT) as a learnable bijection between event sequences and i.i.d.\ unit-rate exponential noise, enabling a single model to be trained across heterogeneous event-stream datasets; (ii) three efficient parameterizations of the cumulative intensity that scale to long sequences; and (iii) a Transformer-based encoder for multi-dataset pretraining. On six real-world benchmarks, SurF achieves the best reported time RMSE on Earthquake, Retweet, and Taobao, and is within trial-level noise of the strongest specialist on the remaining three. Under a strict leave-one-out protocol, the held-out checkpoint beats every classical and neural-autoregressive baseline on 5/6 datasets and beats every baseline on Amazon and Earthquake, an initial step toward foundation models over asynchronous event streams.

</details>


### [18] [Fair and Calibrated Toxicity Detection with Robust Training and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14074)
*Mokshit Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 毒物分类中的公平性需要同时考虑排名、校准和弃权三个维度。训练时干预和后处理安全机制不能独立评估。本文比较了ERM、实例级重加权和Group DRO在这三个维度上的表现，发现：(1)校准差异是隐藏的公平性违规；(2)训练干预只是重新分配而非消除差异；(3)后处理方法继承了训练失败模式；(4)弃权本身就不公平。结论是SRAI公平性需要多维度框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有毒物分类公平性研究通常单独评估训练时干预或后处理安全机制，忽略了二者之间的依赖关系。同时，大多数研究只关注排名指标（如AUC），忽视了校准和弃权维度上的公平性。作者旨在揭示这些被忽视的公平性违规，并论证公平性评估需要多维度框架。

Method: 比较三种训练方法：ERM、实例级重加权和Group DRO。结合三种后处理机制：温度缩放、基于置信度的弃权和按身份阈值优化。使用子组AUC、BPSN/BNSP AUC、误差差距和子组预期校准误差（ECE）进行评估，采用bootstrap置信区间（n=1000）。

Result: (1) ERM全局校准完美（ECE 0.013）但所有身份子组严重校准偏差（+0.029到+0.134）。(2) 重加权ERM提升排名（BPSN AUC +0.06到+0.12）但校准公平性差距恶化高达+0.232；Group DRO消除校准差异但代价是全局校准恶化（ECE 0.118）。(3) 温度缩放因非均匀校准而失败；置信弃权在ERM下有效但在DRO下失效。(4) 置信度弃权对背景内容比对身份提及内容帮助更大，弃权本身不公平。

Conclusion: 毒物分类的公平性需要同时评估排名、校准和弃权三个维度。仅在聚合排名上表现相似的方法在决定实际危害的失败模式上可能存在显著差异。SRAI公平性必须采用多维度框架来全面评估。

Abstract: Fairness in toxicity classification involves three integrated axes: ranking, calibration, and abstention. Training-time interventions and post-hoc safety mechanisms cannot be evaluated independently because the former determines the efficacy of the latter. We compare Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), instance-level reweighting, and Group DRO across these axes, combined with temperature scaling, confidence-based abstention, and per-identity threshold optimization. Evaluation uses subgroup AUC, BPSN/BNSP AUC, error gaps, and per-subgroup Expected Calibration Error (ECE) with bootstrap CIs ($n = 1000$).
  We report four findings. (1) Calibration disparity is a hidden fairness violation. ERM has near-perfect aggregate calibration ($0.013$) but is significantly miscalibrated across all identity subgroups ($+0.029$ to $+0.134$). (2) Training interventions reshape rather than eliminate disparity. Reweighted ERM improves ranking (BPSN AUC $+0.06$ to $+0.12$) but worsens the calibration-fairness gap by up to $+0.232$. Group DRO eliminates calibration disparity but only by becoming uniformly miscalibrated globally (ECE $0.118$). (3) Post-hoc methods inherit training failure modes. Temperature scaling fails because miscalibration is non-uniform. Confidence-based abstention works under ERM but breaks under DRO, where the risk-coverage curve rises with deferral. (4) Abstention itself is unfair. Confidence-based deferral helps background content far more than identity-mentioning content. We argue that SRAI fairness requires a multi-axis framework: methods that differ only in aggregate ranking can differ sharply in failure modes that determine real-world harm.

</details>


### [19] [Rethinking Layer Relevance in Large Language Models Beyond Cosine Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14075)
*Cristian Hinostroza,Rodrigo Toro Icarte,Christ Devia,Andres Carvallo De Ferari,Eugenio Herrera-Berg,Denis Parra,Jorge F Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明余弦相似度是衡量Transformer层重要性的劣质指标，并提出了基于层移除后精度下降的更鲁棒替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型内部机制对开发可解释和优化的架构至关重要，但现有广泛使用的余弦相似度指标在评估层相关性方面存在缺陷，需要更可靠的评估方法。

Method: 通过理论分析证明余弦相似度与层重要性之间不存在可靠关系（低余弦相似度的层仍可能至关重要）；通过多个LLM的实验验证余弦相似度与实际性能下降之间仅为弱到中等相关性；提出使用层移除后模型精度的实际下降作为替代指标。

Result: 理论证明余弦相似度可任意低而层仍至关重要；实证表明余弦相似度与实际性能退化之间相关性弱或中等，导致对Transformer内部机制的误导性解释。

Conclusion: 应放弃将余弦相似度作为层相关性评估的主要指标，采用层移除后精度下降这一虽计算成本高但更准确的指标，这对模型剪枝、轻量化及可解释LLM开发具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized natural language processing. Understanding their internal mechanisms is crucial for developing more interpretable and optimized architectures. Mechanistic interpretability has led to the development of various methods for assessing layer relevance, with cosine similarity being a widely used tool in the field. On this work, we demonstrate that cosine similarity is a poor proxy for the actual performance degradation caused by layer removal. Our theoretical analysis shows that a layer can exhibit an arbitrarily low cosine similarity score while still being crucial to the model's performance. On the other hand, empirical evidence from a range of LLMs confirms that the correlation between cosine similarity and actual performance degradation is often weak or moderate, leading to misleading interpretations of a transformer's internal mechanisms. We propose a more robust metric for assessing layer relevance: the actual drop in model accuracy resulting from the removal of a layer. Even though it is a computationally costly metric, this approach offers a more accurate picture of layer importance, allowing for more informed pruning strategies and lightweight models. Our findings have significant implications for the development of interpretable LLMs and highlight the need to move beyond cosine similarity in assessing layer relevance.

</details>


### [20] [Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Calling Agents in Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14126)
*Marius S. Knorr,Robert Müller,Jan P. Bremer,Nils Schweingruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用强化学习后训练方法（基于CodeAct多轮智能体框架）提升LLM在FHIR医疗数据图结构上的多步推理能力，在FHIR-AgentBench基准上将Qwen3-8B的准确率从50%（o4-mini）提升至77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型LLM智能体在FHIR结构化医疗图上进行多步推理时，常选错资源或违反遍历约束，导致回答质量低。亟需一种能有效提升LLM在结构化临床图上多轮推理能力的方法。

Method: 将FHIR上的推理建模为可查询结构化图上的序列决策问题；实现多轮CodeAct智能体；使用强化学习进行后训练，包含自定义工具集和训练框架；采用LLM Judge提供基于执行结果的奖励信号。

Result: 在FHIR-AgentBench上，RL后训练将答案正确率从50%（o4-mini）提升至77%（Qwen3-8B），且模型更小、成本更低。同时有效保证了数据完整性约束。

Conclusion: 提出了一套端到端的后训练流程（环境构建、框架搭建、模型训练和自定义评估），能够可靠地提升LLM在结构化临床图上的多轮推理能力，优于基于提示的闭模型基线。

Abstract: Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) is the dominant standard for interoperable exchange of healthcare data. In FHIR, electronic health records form a directed graph of resources. Answering clinically meaningful questions over FHIR requires agents to perform multi-step reasoning, filtering, and aggregation across multiple resource types. Prior work shows that even tool-augmented LLM agents (retrieval, code execution, multi-turn planning) often select the wrong resources or violate traversal constraints. We study this problem in the context of FHIR-AgentBench, a benchmark for realistic question answering over real-world hospital data, and frame reasoning on FHIR as a sequential decision-making problem over a queryable structured graph. We implement a multi-turn CodeAct agent and post-train it with reinforcement learning using a custom harness and tools. A LLM Judge provides execution-grounded rewards. Compared to prompt-based, closed-model baselines, RL post-training improves performance while enforcing data-integrity constraints. Empirically, our approach improves answer correctness from 50% (o4-mini) to 77% on FHIR-AgentBench using a smaller and cheaper Qwen3-8B model. We present an end-to-end post-training pipeline (environment building, harness construction, model training and custom evaluation) that reliably improves multi-turn reasoning over structured clinical graphs.

</details>


### [21] [bde: A Python Package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles via MILE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14146)
*Vyron Arvanitis,Angelos Aslanidis,Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: bde是一个基于JAX和MILE采样推理方法的Python包，专注于表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成，提供与scikit-learn兼容的估计器，用于快速训练、高效MCMC采样以及回归和分类任务中的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决表格数据中贝叶斯深度集成方法的易用性和效率问题，提供一个用户友好的Python工具包，使研究人员和从业者能够方便地进行不确定性量化。

Method: 基于JAX的高效实现，采用Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE)采样推理方法，提供与scikit-learn兼容的估计器接口。

Result: 实现了快速训练、高效马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗采样以及回归和分类任务中的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: bde为表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成提供了一个用户友好且高效的Python实现，降低了不确定性量化的使用门槛。

Abstract: bde is a user-friendly Python package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles with a particular focus on tabular data. Built on an efficient JAX implementation of the sampling-based inference method Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE), it provides scikit-learn compatible estimators for fast training, efficient Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, and uncertainty quantification in both regression and classification tasks.

</details>


### [22] [Uncovering Trajectory and Topological Signatures in Multimodal Pediatric Sleep Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14156)
*Scott Ye,Harlin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态掩码自编码器生成的儿童PSG嵌入中的潜在几何结构、拓扑特征和EHR信息，发现PHATE降维坐标、持续同调摘要和临床特征可为睡眠分析任务提供互补的可解释信号，通过线性/MLP模型将AUPRC从0.05-0.31提升至0.14-0.48，并改善了模型校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在儿科睡眠分析中展现出潜力，但其多模态嵌入的潜在结构尚未被充分理解，本文旨在探索这些嵌入中蕴含的会话级诊断信息。

Method: 使用多模态掩码自编码器提取30秒儿科PSG片段的嵌入，通过PHATE降维获取每epoch坐标、持续同调分析嵌入云的拓扑特征、结合EHR信息，构建线性模型和MLP进行可解释的二分类预测。

Result: 几何、拓扑和临床特征提供了互补增益；晚期融合模型表现更优，AUPRC在去饱和、EEG觉醒、低通气和呼吸暂停任务上分别从0.26/0.31/0.09/0.05提升至0.34/0.48/0.22/0.14；全融合模型在四个二进制任务上均取得最佳校准性能。

Conclusion: 潜在几何/拓扑特征与EHR提供互补的可解释信号，有助于改善嵌入表征在极端不平衡数据下的校准性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: While generative models have shown promise in pediatric sleep analysis, the latent structure of their multimodal embeddings remains poorly understood. This work investigates session-wide diagnostic information contained in the sequences of 30-second pediatric PSG epochs embedded by a multimodal masked autoencoder. We test whether augmenting embeddings with PHATE-derived per-epoch coordinates and whole-night movement descriptors, persistent homology summaries of the embedding cloud, and EHR yields task-relevant signals. Simple linear and MLP models, chosen for interpretability rather than state-of-the-art performance, show that geometric, topological, and clinical features each provide complementary gains. For binary predictions, feature importance is task-dependent, and more expressive late-fusion models generally perform better, with AUPRC improving from 0.26 to 0.34 for desaturation, 0.31 to 0.48 for EEG arousal, 0.09 to 0.22 for hypopnea, and 0.05 to 0.14 for apnea. We also report Brier score and Expected Calibration Error, where the full fusion model yields the best calibration across all four binary tasks. Our study reveals that latent geometry/topology and EHR offer complementary, interpretable signals beyond embeddings, improving calibration and robustness under extreme imbalance.

</details>


### [23] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对基于得分匹配学习多项式指数族分布给出了非渐近的样本复杂度分析，证明了样本界与模型维度呈多项式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 得分匹配在高维连续指数族学习中因计算高效而被广泛应用，但其统计性质的理论理解仍然缺乏，现有工作仅给出了渐近样本复杂度界。

Method: 对具有无界支撑的连续指数族分布（特别是多项式形式）使用得分匹配进行结构学习，并推导出非渐近样本复杂度界。

Result: 推导出的样本界与模型维度呈多项式依赖关系，这是首个非渐近的样本复杂度结果。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了得分匹配在非渐近统计理论上的空白，为高维指数族学习提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [24] [CSI-JEPA: Towards Foundation Representations for Ubiquitous Sensing with Minimal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14171)
*Xuanhao Luo,Zhizhen Li,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSI-JEPA，一种自监督预测表示学习框架，用于标签高效的、多任务Wi-Fi感知。它从未标记的CSI样本中学习可复用的时频谱表示，通过预测掩码通道区域的潜在特征实现。在7个真实Wi-Fi感知任务上，相比最先进的监督Transformer，平均准确率提升最高达10.64个百分点，标签节省最高达98.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSI感知模型依赖任务特定的监督训练，每个任务、设备、用户或环境都需要大量标注数据，而实际部署中未标注CSI丰富但标注成本高昂，限制了可扩展性。

Method: CSI-JEPA采用自监督预测表示学习框架，将CSI幅度窗口沿时间和子载波维度进行token化，提出通道变化感知掩码策略，从局部时域和子载波变化更大的区域采样预测目标，通过预测掩码区域的潜在特征进行预训练，预训练后冻结编码器作为骨干，添加轻量级任务特定适配器用于下游任务。

Result: 在7个真实Wi-Fi感知任务上，CSI-JEPA相比最先进的监督Transformer，平均准确率提升最高达10.64个百分点，在匹配预算的设置下标签节省最高达98.0%。

Conclusion: CSI-JEPA通过自监督预训练学习可复用的时频谱表示，显著提升下游感知性能并大幅减少对标注数据的依赖，为多任务Wi-Fi感知提供了一种标签高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI) provides a widely available sensing modality for human and environment perception, but existing CSI sensing models usually rely on task-specific supervised training and require substantial labeled data for each task, device, user, or environment. This limits their scalability in practical deployments where unlabeled CSI is abundant but labeled data is costly to collect. In this paper, we present CSI-JEPA, a self-supervised predictive representation learning framework for label-efficient, multi-task Wi-Fi sensing. CSI-JEPA learns reusable temporal-spectral representations from unlabeled CSI samples by predicting latent features of masked channel regions from visible context. To better match the physical structure of CSI, CSI-JEPA tokenizes channel-response amplitude windows along the time and subcarrier dimensions. It then introduces a channel variation-aware masking strategy that samples predictive targets from regions with stronger local temporal and subcarrier-domain variations. After pretraining, the encoder is frozen and used as a backbone, with lightweight task-specific adapters added for downstream sensing tasks. We evaluate CSI-JEPA on seven real-world Wi-Fi sensing tasks spanning diverse objectives and deployment settings. The results show that CSI-JEPA improves downstream sensing performance over competitive baselines, achieving up to 10.64 percentage points mean accuracy gain over state-of-the-art supervised Transformer and matched-budget label savings of up to 98.0%.

</details>


### [25] [LLMs Know When They Know, but Do Not Act on It: A Metacognitive Harness for Test-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14186)
*Qi Cao,Yufan Wang,Peijia Qin,Shuhao Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于元认知理论（Nelson-Narens框架）的推理控制框架，通过分离监测（FOK预判和JOL后判）与推理过程，将LLM的自监控信号转化为显式的推理控制接口（决定信任、重试或聚合），在不更新参数的情况下显著提升Claude Sonnet-4.6在文本、代码和多模态推理基准上的表现（准确率从48.3提升至56.9），并超越多个榜单领先条目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已经具备自我监测信号（如预估成功率、判断答案正确性），但这些信号通常被孤立地测量或引导，而未被用于控制推理过程。本文旨在探索LLM是否拥有潜在的元认知能力，并将其转化为有效的测试时控制机制。

Method: 受Nelson-Narens认知心理学理论启发，提出元认知控制框架：将监测（monitoring）与推理（reasoning）分离。每个问题先进行预解法的"知晓感"（FOK）信号报告；每次求解尝试后进行"学习判断"（JOL）信号报告。这些信号被转化为显式控制接口：决定信任当前解、使用简洁元认知反馈重试、或将多次尝试传递至最终聚合器。

Result: 在文本、代码和多模态推理基准上，该方法使固定的Claude Sonnet-4.6基础模型在不更新参数且无基准专用微调的情况下，将汇总准确率从48.3提升至56.9，并在HLE-Verified、LiveCodeBench v6和R-Bench-V三个主要评估设置上超过榜单最强条目。

Conclusion: 强大的LLM可能已具备有用的元认知能力，但需要一个显式的控制框架来在推理过程中激活和利用这些能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often expose useful signals of self-monitoring: before solving a problem, they can estimate whether they are likely to succeed, and after solving it, they can judge whether their answer is likely to be correct. However, these signals are typically measured or elicited in isolation, rather than used to control inference. In this work, we ask whether LLMs possess latent metacognitive ability that can be turned into effective test-time control. Inspired by the Nelson--Narens theory from cognitive psychology, we propose a metacognitive harness that separates monitoring from reasoning. For each problem, the model first reports a pre-solve feeling-of-knowing (FOK) signal; after each solve attempt, it reports a post-solve judgment-of-learning (JOL) signal. Rather than treating these signals as passive confidence estimates, the harness turns them into an explicit control interface for reasoning: it decides when to trust the current solution, when to retry with compact metacognitive feedback, and when to pass multiple attempts to a final aggregator. Across text, code, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, our harness substantially improves a fixed Claude Sonnet-4.6 base model without parameter updates or benchmark-specific fine-tuning. On the evaluated public benchmark snapshots, it raises pooled accuracy from 48.3 to 56.9 and exceeds the strongest listed leaderboard entries on the three primary evaluation settings: HLE-Verified, LiveCodeBench v6, and R-Bench-V. These results suggest that strong LLMs may already possess useful metacognitive ability, but require an explicit control harness to act on it during reasoning.

</details>


### [26] [Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210)
*Yingying Fang,Haijie Xu,Shuang Wu,Mariathasan Anish,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度概念瓶颈模型（Fine-grained CBM），通过将每个概念定位到局部视觉证据上，使模型不仅能预测概念，还能验证概念是否对应正确的视觉区域，从而提升可解释性和可信度。实验表明该方法在保持预测性能的同时显著增强了透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）虽然通过引入人类可理解的概念来提高可解释性，但无法验证预测的概念是否对应于正确的视觉证据，容易学习到虚假相关性，限制了模型的可靠性。

Method: 提出一种细粒度CBM框架，将每个概念与局部化的视觉证据相关联，使概念表征能够被直接检查，用户可以解释预测结果并验证模型是否学到了预期的概念而非虚假相关性。

Result: 在医学影像基准上的实验表明，学习到的概念空间是信息完备的，预测性能与标准CBM相当，同时显著提升了透明度。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了可解释性与可验证性之间的鸿沟，增强了CBM的可信度，并为概念层面的人机交互建立了原则性机制，为更可靠、临床可操作的概念学习系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer interpretable alternatives to black-box predictors by introducing human-relatable concepts before the final output. However, existing CBMs struggle to verify whether predicted concepts correspond to the correct visual evidence, limiting their reliability. We propose a fine-grained CBM framework that grounds each concept in localized visual evidence, enabling direct inspection of where and how concepts are encoded. This design allows users to interpret predictions and verify that the model learns intended concepts rather than spurious correlations. Experiments on medical imaging benchmarks show that our learned concept space is information-complete and achieves predictive performance comparable to standard CBMs, while substantially improving transparency. Unlike post-hoc attribution methods, our framework validates both the presence and correctness of concept representations, bridging interpretability with verifiability. Our approach enhances the trustworthiness of CBMs and establishes a principled mechanism for human-model interaction at the concept level, paving the way toward more reliable and clinically actionable concept-based learning systems.

</details>


### [27] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreFT（仅预填充微调），在推理时仅对预填充阶段应用适配器，解码阶段丢弃，相比传统PEFT在多适配器服务场景下吞吐量提升1.9倍（Llama 3.1 70B上512个适配器），且可通过增加秩补偿性能损失，在RL任务中接近标准PEFT水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法在服务多用户个性化适配器时，由于预填充与解码阶段的吞吐量不匹配（解码阶段吞吐量远低于预填充），导致整体服务吞吐量严重下降。现有优化（专用内核、内存管理）无法从根本上解决这一问题。

Method: 提出PreFT（Prefill-only Finetuning），仅在预填充阶段对输入token应用适配器，解码生成时丢弃适配器。基于vLLM推理引擎实现了两种预填充专用PEFT（LoRA和ReFT），并对SFT和RL任务进行了系统评估。

Result: 服务512个适配器时，PreFT吞吐量是传统PEFT的1.9倍。SFT任务中PreFT评估损失略高，但可通过增加适配器秩补偿且几乎不降低吞吐量；RL任务中PreFT与标准PEFT性能接近。联合优化下PreFT在准确率-吞吐量权衡上优于传统PEFT。

Conclusion: 验证了仅预填充适配是一种比现有PEFT更优的准确率-吞吐量权衡方案，适合大规模个性化LLM服务场景。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [28] [Diagnosing Training Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14220)
*Tianle Zhong,Neiwen Ling,Yifan Pi,Zijun Wei,Tianshu Yu,Geoffrey Fox,Peng Wu,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现代LLM强化学习系统中，训练和推理阶段间的实现差异导致令牌级数值不匹配（TIM），这种不匹配会独立引发训练崩溃，并非无害的数值噪声，而是应作为一阶因素考虑的系统级扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM RL系统中训练（rollout生成）与推理（策略优化）两个阶段因实现差异导致令牌概率分配不一致，产生训练-推理不匹配（TIM）问题。该问题与off-policy漂移及常见稳定机制纠缠在一起，难以被检查和诊断。

Method: 设计零不匹配诊断环境（VeXact）将TIM与其它因素隔离，展示小规模令牌级数值不一致可独立引发训练崩溃；分析TIM如何改变有效优化问题，并识别一组缓解措施。

Result: TIM并非无害的数值噪声，而是一种系统级扰动，能够独立导致训练崩溃；TIM改变了有效优化问题；识别了若干可缓解TIM的补救措施。

Conclusion: TIM应被视为分析LLM RL稳定性的一阶因素，而非可忽略的数值误差。

Abstract: Modern LLM RL systems separate rollout generation from policy optimization. These two stages are expected to produce token probabilities that match exactly. However, implementation differences can make them assign different values to the same sequence under the same model weights, inducing Training-Inference Mismatch (TIM). TIM is difficult to inspect because it is entangled with off-policy drift and common stabilization mechanisms. In this work, we isolate TIM in a zero-mismatch diagnostic setting (VeXact), and show that small token-level numerical disagreements can independently cause training collapse. We further show that TIM changes the effective optimization problem, and identify a set of remedies that could mitigate TIM. Our results suggest that TIM is not benign numerical noise, but a systems-level perturbation that should be treated as a first-order factor in analyzing LLM RL stability.

</details>


### [29] [AudioMosaic: Contrastive Masked Audio Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14231)
*Hanxun Huang,Qizhou Wang,Xingjun Ma,Cihang Xie,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AudioMosaic是一种基于对比学习的音频自监督编码器，通过结构化时频掩码构建正样本对，实现高效的大批量训练，在多个音频基准上达到SOTA性能，并能提升音频-语言模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习方法在音频自监督学习中未被充分探索，主要原因在于难以设计有效的音频数据增强方法以及对比预训练所需的大批量尺寸带来的内存开销。

Method: AudioMosaic在预训练阶段通过对频谱图块施加结构化的时频掩码来构建正样本对，从而降低内存使用并实现高效的大批量训练。

Result: 在多个标准音频基准上，在线性探测和微调两种评估协议下均达到了SOTA性能；将预训练编码器集成到音频-语言模型中也能提升后者的任务表现。

Conclusion: 与生成式方法相比，AudioMosaic编码器能够学习更具判别性的语句级表示，并在数据集、领域和声学条件下展现出强迁移能力。

Abstract: Audio self-supervised learning (SSL) aims to learn general-purpose representations from large-scale unlabeled audio data. While recent advances have been driven mainly by generative reconstruction objectives, contrastive approaches remain less explored, partly due to the difficulty of designing effective audio augmentations and the large batch sizes required for contrastive pre-training. We introduce \textbf{AudioMosaic}, a contrastive learning-based audio encoder for general audio understanding. During pre-training, AudioMosaic constructs positive pairs by applying structured time-frequency masking to spectrogram patches, which reduces memory usage and enables efficient large-batch training. Compared with generative approaches, the AudioMosaic encoder learns more discriminative utterance-level representations that demonstrate strong transferability across datasets, domains, and acoustic conditions. Extensive experiments show that AudioMosaic achieves state-of-the-art performance on several standard audio benchmarks under both linear probing and fine-tuning. We further show that integrating the pretrained AudioMosaic encoder into audio-language models improves performance on audio-language tasks. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/AudioMosaic}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [30] [Quantum Advantage in Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14235)
*Simranjeet Singh Dahia,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过CHSH博弈和合作导航任务，实证评估了量子纠缠在量子多智能体强化学习（QMARL）中的协调作用。在CHSH博弈中，纠缠QMARL智能体达到Tsirelson极限0.854，超越经典上限0.75；无纠缠量子电路仅匹配经典基线，证实纠缠是关键机制。在CoopNav中，无纠缠QMARL比经典MAA2C成功率提升约2倍（0.85 vs 0.40），混合架构（量子actor+经典critic）表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QMARL研究缺乏可验证基线，无法严格区分量子优势与算法巧合，亟需在有数学证明的经典基准下进行实证评估。

Method: 采用带有变分量子电路（VQC）actor的去中心化QMARL框架，在CHSH博弈中通过共享纠缠态进行协调，并在合作导航任务中对比全经典、全量子及混合（量子actor+经典critic）架构。

Result: CHSH博弈中纠缠QMARL达到0.854胜率（接近Tsirelson极限），无纠缠量子电路仅0.75（匹配经典基线）；CoopNav中无纠缠QMARL成功率达0.85，混合架构最优。

Conclusion: 纠缠是QMARL实现量子优势的核心机制，不同纠缠结构影响各异；混合量子-经典架构在实际任务中表现突出，为未来QMARL设计提供方向。

Abstract: We present an empirical evaluation of quantum entanglement in agent coordination within quantum multi agent reinforcement learning (QMARL). While QMARL has attracted growing interest recently, most prior work evaluates quantum policies without provable baselines, making it impossible to rigorously distinguish quantum advantage from algorithmic coincidence. We address this directly by evaluating a decentralized QMARL framework with variational quantum circuit (VQC) actors with shared entangled states. In the CHSH game, which has a mathematically proven classical performance ceiling of 0.75 win rate, we show that entangled QMARL agents approach the Tsirelson limit of 0.854, providing clear evidence of their quantum advantage. We show that unentangled quantum circuits match the classical baseline, confirming that entanglement and not the quantum circuit itself is the active coordination mechanism. We also explore the effect of specific entanglement structures, as some Bell states enable coordination gains while others actively harm performance. On cooperative navigation (CoopNav), QMARL without entanglement achieves $\sim2\times$ improvement in success rate over classical MAA2C ($\sim$0.85 versus $\sim$0.40), with the hybrid configuration, quantum actor paired with a classical centralised critic, outperforming both fully classical and fully quantum solutions. We present our experimental analysis and discuss future work.

</details>


### [31] [Active Learners as Efficient PRP Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14236)
*Jeremías Figueiredo Paschmann,Juan Kaplan,Francisco Nattero Santiago Mauricio Barron Bucolo,Juan Wisznia,Luciano del Corro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将PRP重排序重新定义为基于噪声成对比较的主动学习，用主动排序器替代传统排序算法，在调用受限条件下提升NDCG@10；同时引入随机方向预言机将位置偏差转化为零均值噪声，实现无偏聚合排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRP方法依赖经典排序算法聚合LLM的成对偏好判断，但判断存在噪声、顺序敏感性和非传递性，排序假设不匹配实际场景；且排序追求完整排列，截断后无法可靠得到Top-K结果。

Method: 将PRP重排序重构为从噪声成对比较中进行主动学习的问题，使用主动排序器作为即插即用替代方案；提出随机方向预言机，每次比较仅需单次LLM调用，将系统性的位置偏差转化为零均值噪声。

Result: 在调用受限条件下，主动排序器相比传统排序方法显著提升了NDCG@10指标；随机方向预言机实现了无偏聚合排序，消除了双向调用的开销。

Conclusion: 主动学习框架是PRP重排序更有效且噪声鲁棒的方案，随机方向预言机为处理LLM位置偏差提供了高效且无偏的解决方案。

Abstract: Pairwise Ranking Prompting (PRP) elicits pairwise preference judgments from an LLM, which are then aggregated into a ranking, usually via classical sorting algorithms. However, judgments are noisy, order-sensitive, and sometimes intransitive, so sorting assumptions do not match the setting. Because sorting aims to recover a full permutation, truncating it to meet a call budget does not produce a dependable top-K. We thus reframe PRP reranking as active learning from noisy pairwise comparisons and show that active rankers are drop-in replacements that improve NDCG@10 per call in the call-constrained regime. Our noise-robust framework also introduces a randomized-direction oracle that uses a single LLM call per pair. This approach converts systematic position bias into zero-mean noise, enabling unbiased aggregate ranking without the cost of bidirectional calls.

</details>


### [32] [Paraphrasing Attack Resilience of Various AI-Generated Text Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14240)
*Andrii Shportko,Inessa Verbitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种机器生成文本检测方法（微调RoBERTa、Binoculars、文本特征分析及其集成模型）在面对 paraphrasing 攻击时的鲁棒性，发现包含Binoculars的集成模型性能最强但攻击下性能损失也最大，揭示了AI文本检测中性能与韧性之间的二元对立关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM大规模涌现，抄袭和虚假信息传播等问题日益严重，同时AI检测绕过工具也在兴起，亟需可靠的机器生成文本检测方法。现有研究缺乏对检测方法在面对paraphrasing攻击时鲁棒性的系统评估。

Method: 对比评估三种机器生成文本检测方法：微调RoBERTa、Binoculars、文本特征分析，并使用随机森林分类器构建它们的集成模型；通过paraphrasing攻击测试各方法的韧性。

Result: 包含Binoculars的集成模型在正常情况下表现最强，但在遭受paraphrasing攻击时性能下降最为显著，揭示了性能与韧性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的AI文本检测技术在性能与对抗韧性之间存在矛盾关系，强性能的检测方法在面对paraphrasing攻击时反而更脆弱，这一发现挑战了现有对检测方法可靠性的认知。

Abstract: The recent large-scale emergence of LLMs has left an open space for dealing with their consequences, such as plagiarism or the spread of false information on the Internet. Coupling this with the rise of AI detector bypassing tools, reliable machine-generated text detection is in increasingly high demand. We investigate the paraphrasing attack resilience of various machine-generated text detection methods, evaluating three approaches: fine-tuned RoBERTa, Binoculars, and text feature analysis, along with their ensembles using Random Forest classifiers. We discovered that Binoculars-inclusive ensembles yield the strongest results, but they also suffer the most significant losses during attacks. In this paper, we present the dichotomy of performance versus resilience in the world of AI text detection, which complicates the current perception of reliability among state-of-the-art techniques.

</details>


### [33] [Artificial Intelligence-Assistant Cardiotocography: Unified Model for Signal Reconstruction, Fetal Heart Rate Analysis, and Variability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14242)
*Xiaohua Wang,Kai Yu,XuXiao Liang,Liang Wang,Chao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的FHrCTG模型，用于胎儿心率监测。该模型在558,412个未标记数据上预训练，并在7,266个专家审核样本上微调，引入IOL方法将速率分析转为分类判断，在检测关键减速和加速方面表现出高敏感性和特异性，并在Fischer标准验证中取得优异AUC分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统胎儿心率监测方法受限于设备性能、数据传输和医生主观评估，亟需一种能有效抑制噪声、精确重建信号的自动化方法以预防胎儿窘迫和不良妊娠结局。

Method: 开发了基于AI的FHrCTG模型，采用大规模无标签数据预训练+专家标注数据微调的两阶段训练策略；并提出Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL)方法，将连续的速率分析转换为离散的分类判断任务。

Result: 检测关键减速的灵敏度和特异性分别为89.13%和87.78%，检测加速的灵敏度和特异性分别为62.5%和92.04%；基于Fischer标准，验证FHR周期性和振幅变化的AUC分别达到0.7214和0.9643。

Conclusion: 所提出的AI模型能够有效降低噪声干扰并精确重建FHR信号，在临床关键指标的检测上具有高灵敏度和特异性，验证了该方法在胎儿心率监测中的有效性和临床潜力。

Abstract: The monitoring of fetal heart rate (FHR) and the assessment of its variability are crucial for preventing fetal compromise and adverse outcomes. However, traditional methods encounter limitations arising from equipment performance, data transmission, and subjective assessments by doctors. We have developed a tailored AI-based FHrCTG model specifically for FHR monitoring, which effectively mitigates noise interference and precisely reconstructs signals. Our model was pre-trained on a massive dataset consisting of 558,412 unlabeled data points and further refined using 7,266 expert-reviewed entries. To validate FHR, we introduced the Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL) approach, which transforms rate analysis into categorical judgments. Testing revealed that our model demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity in detecting critical FHR decelerations (89.13% and 87.78%, respectively) and accelerations (62.5% and 92.04%, respectively). Furthermore, based on Fischer's criteria for clinical application, our model achieved impressive AUC scores of 0.7214 and 0.9643 for verifying FHR periodicity and amplitude variation, respectively.

</details>


### [34] [Not All Timesteps Matter Equally: Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14252)
*Kai Sun,Peibo Duan,Yongsheng Huang,Guowei Zhang,Benjamin Smith,Nanxu Gong,Levin Kuhlmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出选择性对齐知识蒸馏（SeAl-KD），通过选择性对齐类别级和时间级知识，在错误时间步均衡竞争logits并基于置信度和时间步间相似性重新加权时间对齐，以解决SNN中传统蒸馏方法对所有时间步强加统一对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN与ANN之间存在性能差距，现有知识蒸馏方法默认所有时间步的预测应被平等对待，但SNN预测随时间变化且中间时间步无需单独正确，强制统一对齐会破坏有用的时间动态。

Method: 提出SeAl-KD方法：1) 在错误时间步通过均衡竞争logits进行选择性类别级对齐；2) 基于置信度和时间步间相似性重新加权时间对齐，实现选择性时间级知识蒸馏。

Result: 在静态图像和神经形态事件数据集上，SeAl-KD相较于现有蒸馏方法取得了一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 选择性对齐策略能够更有效地提升SNN性能，保留有益的时间动态，避免传统均匀蒸馏的负面影响。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs), which are brain-inspired and spike-driven, achieve high energy efficiency. However, a performance gap between SNNs and artificial neural networks (ANNs) still remains. Knowledge distillation (KD) is commonly adopted to improve SNN performance, but existing methods typically enforce uniform alignment across all timesteps, either from a teacher network or through inter-temporal self-distillation, implicitly assuming that per-timestep predictions should be treated equally. In practice, SNN predictions vary and evolve over time, and intermediate timesteps need not all be individually correct even when the final aggregated output is correct. Under such conditions, effective distillation should not force every timestep toward the same supervision target, but instead provide corrective guidance to erroneous timesteps while preserving useful temporal dynamics. To address this issue, we propose Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation (SeAl-KD), which selectively aligns class-level and temporal knowledge by equalizing competing logits at erroneous timesteps and reweighting temporal alignment based on confidence and inter-timestep similarity. Extensive experiments on static image and neuromorphic event-based datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over existing distillation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/KaiSUN1/SeAl

</details>


### [35] [Architecture-Aware Explanation Auditing for Industrial Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14255)
*Sibo Jia,Zihang Zhao,Kunrong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"原生读出假设"的解释审计协议，通过扰动试验发现解释方法的忠实度受其与模型决策机制的结构距离约束。在WM-811K晶圆图数据集上，ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout在分类精度较低的情况下，Deletion AUC（0.211）显著优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM（0.432-0.525）。模型无关方法RISE将所有架构压缩至AUC约0.1，表明差距源于解释器通路而非模型架构。研究指出忠实度排名是(模型、解释器、扰动算子)三元组的联合属性，建议根据读出结构协同设计解释通路与模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业视觉检测系统中，深度学习分类器的热力图解释虽视觉上合理，但可能无法识别真正驱动模型决策的图像区域，缺乏可靠的量化评估框架。

Method: 基于原生读出假设，构建架构感知的解释审计协议；在WM-811K（9类172k图像）上采用三随机种子零填充扰动协议，评估多种模型（ViT-Tiny、Swin-Tiny、ResNet18+CBAM、DenseNet121）与解释方法（Attention Rollout、Grad-CAM、RISE）的忠实度；通过模糊填充敏感性分析和MVTec AD边界条件研究验证结论稳健性。

Result: ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout取得Deletion AUC 0.211，显著优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM（0.432-0.525），Cohen's d绝对值>1.1。Swin-Tiny作为Transformer但兼容Grad-CAM，表明操作因素是读出结构而非架构族。RISE将所有模型压缩至AUC约0.1，且优于所有原生方法。模糊填充下排名顺序反转，确认忠实度为三元组联合属性。

Conclusion: 解释通路应与模型架构基于读出结构协同设计；部署的热力图应附带定量忠实度指标；忠实度排名是模型、解释器、扰动算子三者的联合属性，非绝对排序；审计结果具有数据集/任务依赖性。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection systems increasingly rely on deep classifiers whose heatmap explanations may appear visually plausible while failing to identify the image regions that actually drive model decisions. This paper operationalizes an architecture-aware explanation audit protocol grounded in the native-readout hypothesis: the perturbation-based faithfulness of an explanation method is bounded by its structural distance from the model's native decision mechanism. On WM-811K wafer maps (9 classes, 172k images) under a three-seed zero-fill perturbation protocol, ViT-Tiny + Attention Rollout attains Deletion AUC 0.211 against 0.432-0.525 for Swin-Tiny / ResNet18+CBAM / DenseNet121 + Grad-CAM (abs(Cohen's d) > 1.1), despite lower classification accuracy. Swin-Tiny disentangles architecture family from readout structure: despite being a Transformer, its spatial feature-map hierarchy makes it Grad-CAM compatible, showing that the operative factor is readout structure rather than architecture family. A model-agnostic control (RISE) compresses all families to Deletion AUC about 0.1, indicating the gap arises from the explainer pathway; notably, RISE outperforms all native methods, so native readout is a compatibility principle rather than an optimality guarantee. A blur-fill sensitivity analysis shows that the family ordering reverses under a different perturbation baseline, reinforcing that faithfulness rankings are joint properties of (model, explainer, perturbation operator) triples. An exploratory boundary-condition study on MVTec AD (pretrained models) indicates that audit results are dataset/task dependent and identifies conditions requiring qualification. The protocol yields actionable guidance: explanation pathways should be co-designed with model architectures based on readout structure, and deployed heatmaps should be accompanied by quantitative faithfulness metrics.

</details>


### [36] [TILT: Target-induced loss tilting under covariate shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14280)
*Kakei Yamamoto,Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Target-Induced Loss Tilting (TILT)方法，通过分解预测器为f+b并在目标域上惩罚b组件，实现无监督域适应。理论上证明了该方法隐式实现重要性加权，具有有限风险界，实验表明优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决协变量偏移下的无监督域适应问题，传统重要性加权方法在支持集不重叠时失效，需要更鲁棒的域适应方法。

Method: 将源域预测器分解为f+b，在标注源数据上拟合f+b，同时在未标注目标数据上惩罚辅助组件b，最终部署f作为目标预测器。该方法隐式实现自定位的重要性加权。

Result: 证明了总体水平上目标侧惩罚隐式诱导相对重要性加权；导出了有限样本过风险oracle不等式；在控制回归和CIFAR-100蒸馏任务上优于源域训练、精确重要性加权和相对密度比基线方法。

Conclusion: TILT提供了一种稳定且理论上可保证的域适应方法，对正则化参数具有鲁棒性，即使源域和目标域支持集不重叠也能保持有界性。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze Target-Induced Loss Tilting (TILT) for unsupervised domain adaptation under covariate shift. It is based on a novel objective function that decomposes the source predictor as $f+b$, fits $f+b$ on labeled source data while simultaneously penalizing the auxiliary component $b$ on unlabeled target inputs. The resulting fit $f$ is deployed as the final target predictor. At the population level, we show that this target-side penalty implicitly induces relative importance weighting at the population level, but in terms of an estimand $b^*_f$ that is self-localized to the current error, and remains uniformly bounded for any source-target pair (even those with disjoint supports). We prove a general finite-sample oracle inequality on the excess risk, and use it to give an end-to-end guarantee for training with sparse ReLU networks. Experiments on controlled regression problems and shifted CIFAR-100 distillation show that TILT improves target-domain performance over source-only training, exact importance weighting, and relative density-ratio baselines, with a stable dependence on the regularization parameter.

</details>


### [37] [Smooth Multi-Policy Causal Effect Estimation in Longitudinal Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14284)
*Wenxin Chen,Weishen Pan,Kyra Gan,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统纵向因果推断方法独立估计每个动态治疗策略导致有限样本方差膨胀的问题，本文提出策略感知的迭代条件期望Q函数重参数化方法（PEQ-Net），通过共享策略编码器联合估计多个策略，并利用核均值嵌入保证表征空间反映策略差异性，理论上证明该结构能约束二阶余项从而稳定方差，在半合成数据集上显著降低均方根误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的纵向因果推断方法对每个动态治疗策略单独估计，无法跨反事实共享信息，导致即使经过LTMLE标准去偏后仍存在结构性的未控制二阶偏差，增大了有限样本方差。

Method: 提出策略感知的ICE Q函数重参数化方法，设计PEQ-Net架构（中心为共享策略编码器），使用核均值嵌入训练编码器使表征空间反映策略间差异，最后应用LTMLE校正步骤。
